| THEORY_FLOOR_STATEMENT | Known-floor projection is pointwise pinball-nonworsening; true conditional lower inverse quantile has strict breach probability at most alpha, with possible inequality at atoms. | "Let the forecast-origin information include B, let Y≥B almost surely, and let 0&lt;τ&lt;1. Project any issued report c to max(B,c). Its pinball loss is no larger at every possible Y. A true conditional lower inverse quantile q at level τ=1−α has strict breach probability at most α. Equality is not automatic: an atom at the quantile can make the probability smaller. The lower bound alone need not create an outcome atom; fitted reports do not inherit the population statement." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/1/html |
| THEORY_FLOOR_IDENTITY | Exact pinball projection identity and true-quantile strict-tail qualification. | "\\rho_\\tau(Y-c)-\\rho_\\tau(Y-\\max\\{B,c\\})=\\tau(B-c)_+,\\qquad \\Pr(Y>q\\mid\\mathcal F_t)\\le\\alpha." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/2/latex |
| THEORY_FLOOR_PROOF | Projection proof and limitation of equality-based coverage diagnostics. | "Proof. Below B both errors are nonnegative, so their loss difference is the displayed nonnegative quantity; otherwise the report is unchanged. For the conditional inverse quantile, F(q)≥τ, hence the strict-tail probability is at most α. An atom is a possible reason for strict inequality, not a necessary premise. Accordingly, the retained equality-based coverage tests are diagnostics of their stated nulls, not automatic tests of every valid upper quantile." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/3/html |
| THEORY_LEVY_STATEMENT | Classical endpoint-to-peak corollary: independent individually zero-symmetric future real increments, fixed starting anchor, population absolute-displacement quantile. | "Let future real increments be independent and individually symmetric about zero. Write S<sub>h</sub> for their partial sums, S<sub>0</sub>=0, and fix an observed starting position x with B=&#124;x&#124;. No identical-distribution or finite-moment assumption is required. If q is the population (1−α/2) quantile of &#124;S<sub>H</sub>&#124;, the report B+q has strict peak-breach probability at most α. This is a direct scalar application of the classical Lévy maximal inequality, not a new inequality." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/5/html |
| THEORY_LEVY_IDENTITY | Triangle inclusion, factor-two maximal inequality and correct population quantile bound. | "\\Pr\\!\\left(\\max_{0\\le h\\le H}&#124;x+S_h&#124;>B+q\\right)\\le\\Pr\\!\\left(\\max_h&#124;S_h&#124;>q\\right)\\le2\\Pr(&#124;S_H&#124;>q)\\le\\alpha." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/6/latex |
| THEORY_LEVY_CONDITIONAL_PLUGIN_LIMIT | Conditional theorem needs conditional premises and conditional population q; empirical overlapping-data plug-in inherits no automatic theorem guarantee. | "Proof. The triangle inequality gives the first inclusion; Lévy’s maximal inequality and the inverse-quantile tail bound give the rest. For conditional coverage, independence and symmetry must hold conditional on origin information and q must be the corresponding conditional population quantile. The empirical plug-in L, overlapping historical labels and dependent mean-reverting data do not acquire that guarantee. A generic one-sided endpoint VaR is not the required absolute-displacement quantile. Supplement S15 gives an exact finite counterexample to that substitution; Supplement S11 retains the proof, original simulations and corrected sensitivities." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/7/html |
| THEORY_COMMON_POOL_STATEMENT | Construction bounds require common history, common available rank, and peaks including the start; no accuracy ordering. | "Use one nonempty historical pool, one available order-statistic rank k, and peaks that include their starting observation. Let F add historical peak headroom to the current B, S translate each historical signed path to x, and K floor the historical-peak order statistic at B. Their differences obey the following deterministic bounds. The common pool and same rank are essential; these are neither forecast-accuracy nor coverage guarantees." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/9/html |
| THEORY_COMMON_POOL_BOUNDS | Exact common-pool anchor-distance inequalities. | "&#124;F-K&#124;\\le r_{\\rm abs}=\\max_i\\bigl&#124;&#124;N_i&#124;-B\\bigr&#124;,\\qquad &#124;S-K&#124;\\le r_{\\rm signed}=\\max_i&#124;N_i-x&#124;." | notes/revision-v5-novelty/theory-source.json | /blocks/10/latex |

| THEORY_LEVY_INDEPENDENCE_SYMMETRY_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: independence and symmetry | "Independence makes the remaining sum independent of each finite-history first-crossing event; individual zero-symmetry makes its two closed half-axis probabilities at least one half. Equal summand laws and finite moments are unnecessary." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/0/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_STRICT_ATOMS_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: strict event and zero atoms | "For c>=0 a first crossing is strictly positive or strictly negative. A remaining sum with the same weak sign keeps the terminal sum strictly beyond c. Residual atoms at zero do not invalidate the half-probability argument. The same proof covers c=0." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/1/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_QUANTILE_TRIANGLE_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: population quantile and triangle | "For the finite .975 lower quantile q of &#124;S_H&#124;, right continuity yields P(&#124;S_H&#124;>q)<=.025 even at an atom. Since max_h&#124;x+S_h&#124;<=&#124;x&#124;+max_h&#124;S_h&#124;, strict breach of &#124;x&#124;+q has probability at most.05." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/2/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_CONDITIONAL_ISSUE_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: conditional issue interpretation | "The fixed-x population statement is unconditional. A random observed issue anchor becomes fixed only under its issue-information conditional law; the manuscript explicitly requires conditional independence/symmetry and the corresponding conditional endpoint quantile rather than inferring them from unconditional marginals." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/3/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_POPULATION_PLUGIN_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: population versus empirical plugin | "Code uses completed origins r-H-W+1 throughr-H, endpoint sample &#124;N[i+H]-N[i]&#124;, rank ceil(39(W+1)/40), then actual &#124;N[t]&#124; plus the frozen endpoint order statistic. This is a declared empirical plugin, not the known population quantile; dependent overlapping changing samples have no inherited population guarantee." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/4/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_DEPENDENCE_COUNTEREXAMPLE_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: dependence counterexample | "The stated X1=Z, X2=-Z example has symmetric marginals, zero terminal sum and unit maximum, so population q=0 and the strict peak breach is certain. It correctly shows general failure without independence and is separate from the positive-AR experiment." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/5/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_POSITIVE_AR_MECHANISM_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: AR oracle experiment | "Code initializes each path increment at N(0,1), then uses independent Gaussian innovations with variance1-phi^2, so marginal increment variance1 and covariance phi^lag hold. The oracle endpoint variance is the sum of that covariance matrix and its absolute .975 quantile uses normal .9875. Starting x=0 makes this an unconditional population-threshold experiment; finite empirical calibration uncertainty is intentionally absent." | results/revision-20261023/round2/levy-final-assumption-audit.json | /proof_checks/6/agreement |
| THEORY_LEVY_SIMULATION_INTERPRETATION_AUDIT | Existing proof/source consistency audit: interpretation limits | "Positive phi invalidates the independence premise, but need not violate the numerical bound.
